# Extra symbols for things the binary's .dynsym does not name: local (static /
# anonymous-namespace) data, and any function the function map lacks.
#
#   <address> <size> <func|object> <mangled name>
#
# Addresses are absolute (0x71...) like the function map.  The splitter uses
# these names for relocations, so they must be the exact (mangled) names our
# source produces - e.g. a nerve `_ZN12_GLOBAL__N_117FooNrvWaitE` or a C
# static `sMalloc`.

# LibMessageStudio (src/sead/lms/lms_memory.c)
0x7102120908 8 object sMalloc
0x7102120910 8 object sFree

# src/Game/MapObj/BgmStopObj
0x71019F2510 8 object _ZN12_GLOBAL__N_117nrvBgmStopObjWaitE
